=== RUN   TestRunMapsExitCodes/help
amq-squad - role-aware agent team launcher on top of AMQ

Usage:
  amq-squad <command> [options]

Commands:
  roles     List built-in role IDs and market numbers for team creation
  role      Draft and validate reusable custom role personas
  brief     Draft or seed the workstream brief (the only drafter on any launch-capable path)
  team      Set up and manage the team (init, rules, operator, sync)
  task      Task store with leases and pools (add, claim, done, list, reconcile)
  worktree  Plan, materialize, inspect, hand off, and safely clean worker worktrees
  evidence  Run and inspect immutable task-scoped command evidence; ingest hook payloads
  context   Hook verb: the seat's role, rules digest, current task and open gates
  guard     Hook verb: one verdict (allow, needs_gate, deny) for one tool call
  verify    Deterministic preflight checks (action, signed authorization, merge, release)
  gate      Manage durable typed authorization requests (raise and close)
  operator  Inspect or act as the configured operator participant
  status    Show the team's live state in one workstream (--project, --profile, --session)
  fleet     The fleet registry ~/.amq-squad/fleet.yaml (list, add, remove, operator)
  dispatch  Queue a durable task for a child
  amq       Show the resolved AMQ context and explain routes (env, route)
  doctor    Check amq-squad / AMQ setup (use --project and --profile for other teams)
  version   Print the amq-squad version

Global flags (accepted before or after the subcommand, until a literal "--"):
  --quiet              Suppress non-data success/progress notices.
  --verbose            Print additional diagnostic detail.
  --color auto|always|never
                       Control ANSI color output (default auto; honors NO_COLOR).

Exit codes:
  0  success
  1  usage / user error (unknown flag, bad argument, missing required input)
  2  system / runtime error (IO, process, config, environment)
  3  partial success (some targets succeeded, some failed)

Examples:
  amq-squad roles
  amq-squad team init --roles cto,fullstack --binary cto=codex
  amq-squad team init --profile review --roles cto,qa
  amq-squad status --project ~/Code/app --session issue-435 --json
  amq-squad dispatch --session issue-96 --role qa --subject "Validate" --body-file task.md
  amq-squad task list --session issue-96
  amq-squad context --me cto --json
  amq-squad guard --me cto --stdin --json < pretooluse.json
  amq-squad doctor --project ~/Code/app --profile review --json | jq .

Run 'amq-squad <command> --help' for command-specific options.

=== RUN   TestRunMapsExitCodes/unknown_flag_on_doctor_->_usage
flag provided but not defined: -banana
amq-squad doctor - check this project's amq-squad / AMQ setup

Usage:
  amq-squad doctor [--project DIR] [--profile NAME] [--session NAME] [--json]

Checks: the AMQ version, launch API, adoption floor, module and identity pins,
AMQ ops diagnostics, the amq-squad on PATH vs this build (version skew), the
selected team profile, and the worktree plan of the selected workstream.
Read-only. Exits non-zero if any check is "fail".

Examples:
  amq-squad doctor
  amq-squad doctor --project ~/Code/app
  amq-squad doctor --profile review
  amq-squad doctor --json | jq '.data.checks[] | select(.status=="fail")'
